— Completing the Square  [LIVE]  diff 4  type:exact  tol:—
  id: 48d6b11f-ad99-4ed7-8ca4-4e505767022f
  template: <p>Write <strong>x² + {{2 * a}}x + {{b}}</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>
  answer_t: {{b - a * a >= 0 ? "(x + " + a + ")² + " + (b - a * a) : "(x + " + a + ")² − " + Math.abs(b - a * a)}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 10x + 9</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 5)² − 16"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 8x + 20</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 4)² + 4"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 2x + 12</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 1)² + 11"
   trap 1: (x + {{2 * a}})² + {{b}}
   trap 2: (x + {{a}})² + {{b + a * a}}
   expl_t: Halve the x coefficient: (x + {{a}})² = x² + {{2*a}}x + {{a*a}}. <br>Then adjust the constant: x² + {{2*a}}x + {{b}} = {{b - a * a >= 0 ? "(x + " + a + ")² + " + (b - a * a) : "(x + " + a + ")² − " + Math.abs(b - a * a)}}

— Finding the nth Term  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:expression  tol:—
  id: 26e0949b-c952-4e9e-9134-db6a6cb6a99e
  template: <p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>{{a + b}}, {{a + 2*b}}, {{a + 3*b}}, {{a + 4*b}}, ...</strong></p>
  answer_t: {{b}}n + {{a}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>2, -5, -12, -19, ...</strong></p>"  → A="-7n + 9"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>2, -5, -12, -19, ...</strong></p>"  → A="-7n + 9"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>-7, -6, -5, -4, ...</strong></p>"  → A="n - 8"
   trap 1: {{b}}n
   trap 2: {{b}}n + {{a + b}}
   expl_t: Common difference = {{b}}, so nth term starts with {{b}}n. <br>When n=1: {{b}} × 1 = {{b}}. However, the first term is {{a + b}}, so you still need to add {{a}}. <br>The nth term = {{b}}n + {{a}}.

— Graph Transformations  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:exact  tol:—
  id: d64bba55-86e1-4824-b9a5-51301a42a9a6
  template: <p>The point <strong>P({{a}}, {{b}})</strong> lies on the graph of <strong>y = f(x)</strong>.</p><p>The graph is transformed to <strong>y = {{ t==0 ? "−f(x)" : t==1 ? "f(x) + " + k : t==2 ? "f(x) − " + k : t==3 ? "f(x + " + k + ")" : "f(x − " + k + ")" }}</strong>.</p><p>Write down the new coordinates of point P in the form (x, y).</p>
  answer_t: ({{ t==3 ? a-k : t==4 ? a+k : a }}, {{ t==0 ? -b : t==1 ? b+k : t==2 ? b-k : b }})
   · sample 1: Q="<p>The point <strong>P(2, 2)</strong> lies on the graph of <strong>y = f(x)</strong>.</p><p>The graph is transformed to <strong>y = f(x) + 2</strong>.</p><p>Write down the new coordinates of point P in the form (x, y).</p>"  → A="(2, 4)"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>The point <strong>P(2, 4)</strong> lies on the graph of <strong>y = f(x)</strong>.</p><p>The graph is transformed to <strong>y = f(x + 2)</strong>.</p><p>Write down the new coordinates of point P in the form (x, y).</p>"  → A="(0, 4)"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>The point <strong>P(6, 5)</strong> lies on the graph of <strong>y = f(x)</strong>.</p><p>The graph is transformed to <strong>y = f(x) + 4</strong>.</p><p>Write down the new coordinates of point P in the form (x, y).</p>"  → A="(6, 9)"
   trap 1: ({{ t==3 ? a+k : t==4 ? a-k : a }}, {{ t==0 ? b : t==1 ? b-k : t==2 ? b+k : b }})
   trap 2: ({{ t==0 ? -a : t==1 ? a+k : t==2 ? a-k : a }}, {{ t==3 ? b-k : t==4 ? b+k : b }})
   expl_t: {{ t==0 ? "Reflecting in the x-axis (y = −f(x)) negates the y-coordinate and leaves x unchanged." : t==1 ? "y = f(x) + " + k + " shifts the graph up by " + k + ", adding " + k + " to the y-coordinate." : t==2 ? "y = f(x) − " + k + " shifts the graph down by " + k + ", subtracting " + k + " from the y-coordinate." : t==3 ? "y = f(x + " + k + ") shifts the graph " + k + " to the LEFT (a + inside the bracket moves it left), subtracting " + k + " from the x-coordinate." : "y = f(x − " + k + ") shifts the graph " + k + " to the RIGHT, adding " + k + " to the x-coordinate." }}<br>So P({{a}}, {{b}}) → <strong>({{ t==3 ? a-k : t==4 ? a+k : a }}, {{ t==0 ? -b : t==1 ? b+k : t==2 ? b-k : b }})</strong>.

— Inverse Functions  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: b6a855a2-beaf-4f28-879e-448b53296b72
  template: <p>The function f is defined as f(x) = {{a}}x + {{b}}.</p><p>Find <strong>f⁻¹({{a*n + b}})</strong>.</p>
  answer_t: {{n}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>The function f is defined as f(x) = 5x + 8.</p><p>Find <strong>f⁻¹(33)</strong>.</p>"  → A="5"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>The function f is defined as f(x) = 5x + 1.</p><p>Find <strong>f⁻¹(21)</strong>.</p>"  → A="4"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>The function f is defined as f(x) = 3x + 7.</p><p>Find <strong>f⁻¹(19)</strong>.</p>"  → A="4"
   trap 1: {{a*(a*n+b) + b}}
   trap 2: {{round((a*n + b) / a, 4)}}
   expl_t: To find f⁻¹, rearrange y = {{a}}x + {{b}} for x:<br>y − {{b}} = {{a}}x<br>x = (y − {{b}}) ÷ {{a}}<br>So f⁻¹(x) = (x − {{b}}) ÷ {{a}}<br>f⁻¹({{a*n+b}}) = ({{a*n+b}} − {{b}}) ÷ {{a}} = {{a*n}} ÷ {{a}} = <strong>{{n}}</strong>

— Dividing Fractions  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:fraction  tol:—
  id: 1dbb9708-70f7-41cd-ba33-cfe35b0a2f80
  template: <p>Calculate, simplifying your answer fully:</p><p style="font-size:1.3em;text-align:center;margin:12px 0;">{{frac(a, b)}} ÷ {{frac(c, d)}}</p>
  answer_t: {{(a*d)/gcd(a*d,b*c)}}/{{(b*c)/gcd(a*d,b*c)}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Calculate, simplifying your answer fully:</p><p style="font-size:1.3em;text-align:center;margin:12px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">4</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">2</span></span> ÷ <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">1</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">6</span></span></p>"  → A="12/1"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Calculate, simplifying your answer fully:</p><p style="font-size:1.3em;text-align:center;margin:12px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">1</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">4</span></span> ÷ <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">1</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">3</span></span></p>"  → A="3/4"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Calculate, simplifying your answer fully:</p><p style="font-size:1.3em;text-align:center;margin:12px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">3</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">2</span></span> ÷ <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">2</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">4</span></span></p>"  → A="3/1"
   trap 1: {{(a*c)/gcd(a*c,b*d)}}/{{(b*d)/gcd(a*c,b*d)}}
   trap 2: {{(b*c)/gcd(a*d,b*c)}}/{{(a*d)/gcd(a*d,b*c)}}
   expl_t: To divide fractions, multiply by the reciprocal of the divisor:<br>{{frac(a, b)}} ÷ {{frac(c, d)}} = {{frac(a, b)}} × {{frac(d, c)}} = {{frac(a*d, b*c)}}{{gcd(a*d,b*c) > 1 ? " = " + frac(a*d/gcd(a*d,b*c), b*c/gcd(a*d,b*c)) : ""}}

   · sample 1: Q="<p>Write the mixed number <strong>2 <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">6</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">5</span></span></strong> as an improper fraction.</p>"  → A="16/5"

— Percentage Change  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0.01
  id: 3a2a4da5-2798-45a2-9386-7449f4f7139a
  template: <p>Decrease <strong>£{{a}}</strong> by <strong>{{b}}%</strong>.</p>

   · sample 2: Q="<p>Decrease <strong>£36</strong> by <strong>42%</strong>.</p>"  → A="£20.88"

   expl_t: {{b}}% of £{{a}} = £{{(a * b / 100).toFixed(2)}}. Subtract from original: £{{a}} - £{{(a * b / 100).toFixed(2)}} = £{{(a * (1 - b/100)).toFixed(2)}}

   · sample 1: Q="<p>A fair spinner has <strong>6 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 6.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>5 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 5.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/30"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A fair spinner has <strong>8 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 8.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>5 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 5.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/40"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A fair spinner has <strong>8 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 8.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>5 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 5.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/40"
   trap 1: {{a+b}}/{{a*b}}
   trap 2: 1/{{a}}
   trap 3: 1/{{b}}
   expl_t: The two spinners are independent, so multiply their probabilities:<br>P(1 on first spinner) = {{frac(1, a)}}<br>P(1 on second spinner) = {{frac(1, b)}}<br>P(1 on both) = {{frac(1, a)}} × {{frac(1, b)}} = {{frac(1, a*b)}}

— Conditional Probability  [LIVE]  diff 4  type:fraction  tol:—
  id: 6ed2d883-7e79-4c79-ad35-1a462b4a5ab5
  template: <p>A bag contains <strong>{{r}} red</strong> balls and <strong>{{b}} blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>
  answer_t: {{fracStr(r*(r-1),(r+b)*(r+b-1))}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>4 red</strong> balls and <strong>6 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="2/15"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>3 red</strong> balls and <strong>6 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/12"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>2 red</strong> balls and <strong>4 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/15"
   trap 1: {{fracStr(r*r,(r+b)*(r+b))}}
   trap 2: {{fracStr(r,r+b)}}
   expl_t: P(1st ball red) = {{frac(r, r+b)}}<br>After removing one red ball: {{r-1}} red balls remain out of {{r+b-1}} total.<br>P(2nd ball red | 1st was red) = {{frac(r-1, r+b-1)}}<br>P(both red) = {{frac(r, r+b)}} × {{frac(r-1, r+b-1)}} = {{frac(r*(r-1), (r+b)*(r+b-1))}}

— Expected Outcomes  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 8c65d36c-00c3-400a-932c-2cf2e132e273
  template: <p>A bag contains <strong>{{p}} red</strong> counters and <strong>{{10-p}} blue</strong> counters.</p><p>A counter is chosen at random, its colour is recorded, and it is <strong>replaced</strong>.</p><p>This is repeated <strong>{{n*10}} times</strong> in total.</p><p>How many times would you <strong>expect</strong> to pick a red counter?</p>
  answer_t: {{p*n}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>2 red</strong> counters and <strong>8 blue</strong> counters.</p><p>A counter is chosen at random, its colour is recorded, and it is <strong>replaced</strong>.</p><p>This is repeated <strong>20 times</strong> in total.</p><p>How many times would you <strong>expect</strong> to pick a red counter?</p>"  → A="4"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>3 red</strong> counters and <strong>7 blue</strong> counters.</p><p>A counter is chosen at random, its colour is recorded, and it is <strong>replaced</strong>.</p><p>This is repeated <strong>50 times</strong> in total.</p><p>How many times would you <strong>expect</strong> to pick a red counter?</p>"  → A="15"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>1 red</strong> counters and <strong>9 blue</strong> counters.</p><p>A counter is chosen at random, its colour is recorded, and it is <strong>replaced</strong>.</p><p>This is repeated <strong>40 times</strong> in total.</p><p>How many times would you <strong>expect</strong> to pick a red counter?</p>"  → A="4"
   trap 1: {{p*n*10}}
   trap 2: {{n*10}}
   trap 3: {{n*p*10/(10-p)}}
   trap 4: {{p/10}}
   expl_t: P(red) = {{frac(p, 10)}}<br>Expected outcomes = P(red) × number of trials<br>= {{frac(p, 10)}} × {{n*10}} = <strong>{{p*n}}</strong>

— Mutually Exclusive Events  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0.005
  id: 7168c1d6-e36e-4f79-bead-c74bb24ad267
  template: <p>P(A) = <strong>{{a / 10}}</strong> and P(B) = <strong>{{b / 10}}</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>
  answer_t: {{(a + b) / 10}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.2</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.1</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.3"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.4</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.2</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.6"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.3</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.4</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.7"
   trap 1: {{round((a / 10) * (b / 10), 4)}}
   trap 2: {{round((a + b) / 10 - (a / 10) * (b / 10), 4)}}
   expl_t: Mutually exclusive events cannot both occur, so P(A and B) = 0.<br>P(A or B) = P(A) + P(B) = {{a / 10}} + {{b / 10}} = {{(a + b) / 10}}.

— Range  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 98b2ed04-0264-48d7-bd8d-1ea3cba6b6e2
  template: <p>Find the range of these numbers:</p><p><strong>{{d}}, {{a}}, {{e}}, {{b}}, {{c}}</strong></p>

— Compound Units  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 1785da89-1ef2-4b5d-99b8-3a688b566a3e
  template: <p>A train travels <strong>{{5*a * b}} km</strong> at a constant speed of <strong>{{b*5}} km/h</strong>. How long does the journey take in hours?</p>
  answer_t: {{a}} hours
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A train travels <strong>190 km</strong> at a constant speed of <strong>95 km/h</strong>. How long does the journey take in hours?</p>"  → A="2 hours"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A train travels <strong>200 km</strong> at a constant speed of <strong>40 km/h</strong>. How long does the journey take in hours?</p>"  → A="5 hours"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A train travels <strong>240 km</strong> at a constant speed of <strong>60 km/h</strong>. How long does the journey take in hours?</p>"  → A="4 hours"
   trap 1: {{5*a * b}}
   trap 2: {{5*5*a * b * b}}
   trap 3: {{1/a}}
   expl_t: Time = distance ÷ speed.<br>Time = {{5*a * b}} ÷ {{5*b}} = {{a}} hours.

— Growth and Decay  [LIVE]  diff 4  type:numeric  tol:0.01

   · sample 1: Q="<p>£<strong>3000</strong> is invested at <strong>7%</strong> compound interest per year.<br>What is it worth after <strong>4 years</strong>? Give your answer to the nearest penny.</p>"  → A="£3932.39"

   trap 1: {{a * 1000 + a * 1000 * r * n / 100}}
   trap 2: {{a * 1000 * r * n / 100}}

— Proportion with Powers  [LIVE]  diff 4  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 10933441-1407-479d-92f0-5c53ff2392fe
  template: <p>y is proportional to x².<br>When <strong>x = {{a}}</strong>, <strong>y = {{a * a * k}}</strong>.<br>Find <strong>y</strong> when <strong>x = {{b}}</strong>.</p>
  answer_t: {{b * b * k}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>y is proportional to x².<br>When <strong>x = 5</strong>, <strong>y = 50</strong>.<br>Find <strong>y</strong> when <strong>x = 7</strong>.</p>"  → A="98"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>y is proportional to x².<br>When <strong>x = 4</strong>, <strong>y = 48</strong>.<br>Find <strong>y</strong> when <strong>x = 8</strong>.</p>"  → A="192"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>y is proportional to x².<br>When <strong>x = 5</strong>, <strong>y = 150</strong>.<br>Find <strong>y</strong> when <strong>x = 10</strong>.</p>"  → A="600"
   trap 1: {{b * k}}
   trap 2: {{a * a * k + (b - a) * k}}
   trap 3: {{b * b * b * k}}
   expl_t: y = kx². <br>
Find k: <br>
k = y ÷ x² = {{a * a * k}} ÷ {{a}}² = {{a * a * k}} ÷ {{a * a}} = {{k}}.<br>
When x = {{b}}: y = {{k}} × {{b}}² = {{k}} × {{b * b}} = {{b * b * k}}.

— Ratio  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 2471f835-e678-4793-9f29-bcd3d94724a8
  template: <p>Share <strong>£{{(a + b) * c}}</strong> in the ratio <strong>{{a}} : {{b}}</strong>. What is the larger share?</p>
  answer_t: £{{b * c}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Share <strong>£187</strong> in the ratio <strong>3 : 8</strong>. What is the larger share?</p>"  → A="£136"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Share <strong>£228</strong> in the ratio <strong>4 : 8</strong>. What is the larger share?</p>"  → A="£152"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Share <strong>£192</strong> in the ratio <strong>3 : 9</strong>. What is the larger share?</p>"  → A="£144"
   trap 1: {{a * c}}
   trap 2: {{(a + b) * c / 2}}
   trap 3: {{b}}
   trap 4: {{c}}
   expl_t: Total parts = {{a}} + {{b}} = {{a + b}}.<br>Each part is worth £{{(a + b) * c}} ÷ {{a + b}} = £{{c}}.<br>Larger share = {{b}} × £{{c}} = £{{b * c}}.

— Ratio  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:exact  tol:—
  id: 9e709cea-54e8-4d83-a31d-b98ff17b3e82
  template: <p>Simplify the ratio <strong>{{a * b}} : {{a * c}}</strong>.</p>
  answer_t: {{b}} : {{c}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Simplify the ratio <strong>6 : 4</strong>.</p>"  → A="3 : 2"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Simplify the ratio <strong>25 : 15</strong>.</p>"  → A="5 : 3"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Simplify the ratio <strong>18 : 63</strong>.</p>"  → A="2 : 7"
   trap 1: {{a * b}} : {{a * c}}
   trap 2: {{c}} : {{b}}
   trap 3: 1 : {{c / b}}
   expl_t: Find the HCF of {{a * b}} and {{a * c}}, which is {{a}}.<br>Divide both parts by {{a}}: {{a * b}} ÷ {{a}} = {{b}} and {{a * c}} ÷ {{a}} = {{c}}.<br>The simplified ratio is {{b}} : {{c}}.

— Reverse Percentage  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0.01
  id: 4481278a-14a9-479b-892b-703af0d87e97
  template: <p>After a <strong>{{b}}%</strong> increase, a price is <strong>£{{c}}</strong>. What was the original price?</p>
  answer_t: £{{Math.round(c / (1 + b/100) * 100) / 100}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>After a <strong>37%</strong> increase, a price is <strong>£58</strong>. What was the original price?</p>"  → A="£42.34"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>After a <strong>17%</strong> increase, a price is <strong>£144</strong>. What was the original price?</p>"  → A="£123.08"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>After a <strong>29%</strong> increase, a price is <strong>£54</strong>. What was the original price?</p>"  → A="£41.86"
   trap 1: {{c - c * b / 100}}
   trap 2: {{c + c * b / 100}}
   expl_t: Divide by the multiplier: £{{c}} ÷ (1 + {{b/100}}) = £{{c}} ÷ {{1 + b/100}} = £{{Math.round(c / (1 + b/100) * 100) / 100}}

— Angles in Polygons  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: b5edccf8-9acb-4bd7-b45d-ff94000329f1
  template: <p>Find the <strong>sum of interior angles</strong> of a <strong>{{n}}-sided polygon</strong>.</p>

   · sample 2: Q="<p>Find the <strong>sum of interior angles</strong> of a <strong>10-sided polygon</strong>.</p>"  → A="1440"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Find the <strong>sum of interior angles</strong> of a <strong>7-sided polygon</strong>.</p>"  → A="900"
   trap 1: {{n * 180}}
   trap 2: {{n * 90}}
   expl_t: Sum of interior angles of an n-sided polygon = (n − 2) × 180°<br>= ({{n}} − 2) × 180°<br>= {{n - 2}} × 180°<br>= {{(n - 2) * 180}}°

— Area of a Trapezium  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 683e0245-2cbf-4b91-900f-a44a39f3bd03

The distance OT = <strong>20 cm</strong>.</p><p>Find the length of the tangent PT.</p>"  → A="16 cm"
   trap 1: {{5*k - 3*k}}
   trap 2: {{round(Math.sqrt((5*k)*(5*k) + (3*k)*(3*k)), 2)}}
   expl_t: The tangent is perpendicular to the radius at P, so angle OPT = 90°.<br>By Pythagoras: PT² = OT² − OP²<br>= {{5*k}}² − {{3*k}}²<br>= {{25*k*k}} − {{9*k*k}}<br>= {{16*k*k}}<br>PT = √{{16*k*k}} = {{4*k}} cm

— Circumfrence of a Circle  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:numeric  tol:0.5
  id: 1c438666-4afa-4ceb-8c1d-219a6c0b6d83
  template: <p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>{{r}} cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>
  answer_t: {{round(2 * Math.PI * r, 2)}} cm
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>9 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="56.55 cm"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>7 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="43.98 cm"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>6 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="37.7 cm"
   trap 1: {{round(Math.PI * r * r, 2)}}
   trap 2: {{round(Math.PI * r, 2)}}
   trap 3: {{round(2 * Math.PI * 2 * r, 2)}}
   expl_t: Circumference = 2πr<br>= 2 × π × {{r}}<br>≈ {{round(2 * Math.PI * r, 2)}} cm

— Exterior Angles  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: c0c02057-d8d1-43d1-adf0-4ecc5ee570cf
  template: <p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>{{n}} sides</strong>.</p>
<p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>
  answer_t: {{360 / n}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>4 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="90"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>4 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="90"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>12 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="30"
   trap 1: {{(n - 2) * 180 / n}}
   trap 2: {{180 - 360 / n}}
   expl_t: Exterior angle of a regular polygon = 360° ÷ n<br>
= 360° ÷ {{n}}<br>
= {{360 / n}}°

≈ {{round(s * Math.sin(B * Math.PI / 180) / Math.sin(A * Math.PI / 180), 2)}} cm

— Surface Area of a Cone  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0.5
  id: 0799c17c-b016-4728-ba84-91e1239244f7
  template: <div style="text-align:center;margin:4px 0 16px"><svg viewBox="0 0 220 172" style="width:100%;max-width:240px;display:block;margin:0 auto" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M35,145 A75,18 0 0 0 185,145" fill="none" stroke="#374151" stroke-width="1.3" stroke-dasharray="5 3"/><line x1="110" y1="25" x2="35"  y2="145" stroke="#374151" stroke-width="1.8"/><line x1="110" y1="25" x2="185" y2="145" stroke="#374151" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M35,145 A75,18 0 0 1 185,145" fill="none" stroke="#374151" stroke-width="1.8"/>
<line x1="110" y1="145" x2="185" y2="145" stroke="#374151" stroke-width="1.3" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>
<text x="160" y="84"  text-anchor="start"  font-size="13" fill="#374151" font-style="italic" font-family="system-ui,-apple-system,sans-serif">{{l}} cm</text>
<text x="147" y="156" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" fill="#374151" font-style="italic" font-family="system-ui,-apple-system,sans-serif">{{r}} cm</text></svg></div>
